# Independent intake cross-check and alternative-source retry

Authorized reader report for the owner, 2026-10-31. The independent status was written at the timestamp recorded in [phase_boundary.json](phase_boundary.json), before any file under `intake_check/` was opened. The earlier report was then read for Phase B. No subagents were used. No benchmark, pipeline run, docking job or GPU-box connection was launched.

## Result

**Materials remain unusable under the requested provenance rules.** The protein inputs are reachable and complete. A public third-party FroDock package, including the needed binaries, compilation script and scoring potential, was retrieved successfully, but no authoritative checksum or matching official/archive/author-deposit copy was established. It therefore remains an **`unverified_mirror`**, which cannot satisfy the materials prerequisite. No `BRIEF_run5.md` was written; `BRIEF.md` remains unchanged.

The independent prerequisite is **data**, meaning the full required material dependency including the docking software and distributed scoring input; the underlying cause is **source_moved**. Unknown licence evidence is not an intake blocker under the current rule. The dead official distribution path remains the cause even though a third-party candidate can now be downloaded.

Owner action: **supply authenticated materials**—the required FroDock distribution and its scoring potential, or an authoritative upstream checksum/reference copy that can authenticate the retrieved package. If acquisition requires an agreement or account, the owner must handle that step. A decision to use another baseline/version would change the comparator and was not made here. No contact was made with authors or distributors.

Reachability from the GPU box was **not tested**. No claim is made about successful installation, executable compatibility, runtime, scientific reproduction, licence or permission.

## Phase A: independent comparison and original locations

Detailed independent findings and source-file references are in [PHASE_A.md](PHASE_A.md). Important evidence:

- [run1 data](../run1/agent/intake/data.json) and [run3 data](../run3/agent/intake/data.json) marked the protein distribution available based on published retrieval interfaces and HEAD responses. Their own [run1 baseline](../run1/agent/intake/baseline.json) and [run3 baseline](../run3/agent/intake/baseline.json) still report FroDock acquisition failure.
- [run2 data](../run2/agent/intake/data.json) and [run4 data](../run4/agent/intake/data.json) included the external software among required inputs, and also withheld completeness claims. Their partial labels reflect a broader materials scope and a stricter completeness test. They do not establish a change in protein-data reachability.
- All four draws recorded failure of the same acquisition page named by the [pinned PINDER methods README](https://github.com/pinder-org/pinder/blob/ebeb28a2f8411363cef722b7d293c978f7442672/src/pinder-methods/pinder/methods/README.md). The [pinned Dockerfile](https://github.com/pinder-org/pinder/blob/ebeb28a2f8411363cef722b7d293c978f7442672/docker/frodock/Dockerfile.frodock) expects an external archive containing `frodock3_linux64` and `compile_frodock.sh`; the [adapter](https://github.com/pinder-org/pinder/blob/ebeb28a2f8411363cef722b7d293c978f7442672/src/pinder-methods/pinder/methods/run/frodock/predict.py) requires `soap.bin` and MPI/GNU docking, grid, clustering and view tools. The evaluator by itself does not produce predictions.
- The run4 single-anchor output is another difference in recorded evidence, not the missing physical dependency. Published 250-case and 93-paired-apo counts were independently verified from the index. No anchors, scale or selection protocol were changed.

The named FroDock page, its corrected-spelling variant, and the old downloads page each returned 404 on the initial attempt and three retries. Retries started about 60 seconds after the preceding attempt ended. For the exact original page, successful curl transactions returned the same 13,631-byte error body every time. This supports a persistent dead path rather than a transient network failure; Phase B confirms site restructuring. The complete UTC attempt ledger follows below.

### Completeness and integrity established now

The checked original dataset locations were `https://pinderdata.org/2024-02/` and its published `pdbs/`, `test_set_pdbs/`, `mappings/` directories. The index contains 250 PINDER-S cases, exactly 93 with both primary apo monomers. Index and metadata each have 2,319,564 rows; every PINDER-S ID has metadata.

Coverage was broader than the prioritized 93 solely to verify materials, without changing the study:

| Coverage | Result |
| --- | --- |
| All 250 PINDER-S native dimers, separated holo partners, available primary apo partners and their mappings | 1,618 unique files; all reachable, parsed nonempty, exact publisher size/MD5 matches |
| Default `PinderSystem` eager downloads of predicted monomers and alternative apo pairings for the prioritized 93 | 1,972 additional unique files; all reachable, parsed nonempty, exact publisher size/MD5 matches; these were not substituted for primary apo inputs |
| Complete official test ZIP | 181,968,169 compressed bytes; 3,914 files plus one directory; 730,945,215 uncompressed bytes; all ZIP CRCs valid |
| Original AAV mmCIF files 6IHB / 6JCQ / 6JCS | 551,618 / 529,158 / 527,195 bytes; matching entry IDs; nonempty parsed structures with 4,868 / 4,809 / 4,880 atoms |
| PINDER source at recorded commit | 22,835,334-byte source archive retrieved; source inspected without installation/execution |

Files: [input_summary.json](input_summary.json), [input_inventory.json](input_inventory.json), [api_extra_summary.json](api_extra_summary.json), [api_extra_inventory.json](api_extra_inventory.json), [checksum_verification.json](checksum_verification.json), [selected_published_checksums.json](selected_published_checksums.json). The case inventories are [selected_cases.csv](selected_cases.csv) and [pinder_s_cases.csv](pinder_s_cases.csv).

The original publisher's root and directory manifests were fetched from `https://pinderdata.org/2024-02/manifests/{root,pdbs,mappings,test_set_pdbs}.jsonl.gz`, following the [pinned downloader's manifest convention](https://github.com/pinder-org/pinder/blob/ebeb28a2f8411363cef722b7d293c978f7442672/src/pinder-core/pinder/core/utils/dataset.py). All required file sizes and MD5 values matched. The root index, metadata and test ZIP also match their published MD5 values, independently of multipart ETags. The full 169-GB PDB archive and 7.2-GB mapping archive received HEAD checks only; they are optional bulk distribution containers, and every needed member was downloaded individually instead. Training inputs were not needed or downloaded.

The RCSB downloads have locally computed SHA-256 values, complete successful transfers and parse checks; no independent published cryptographic checksum was established for these current mmCIF responses. PDB IDs do not freeze historical revisions. Neither this nor the current PINDER release label proves byte identity with an unspecified historical publication snapshot. Assembly/chain/domain extraction and preregistered candidate/seed choices remain study preparation, not absent source files. These were not invented or changed.

## Agreement and disagreement with the earlier Opus reader

The earlier [REPORT.md](../intake_check/REPORT.md) was first opened after [status_independent.json](status_independent.json) existed.

Agreement:

- The primary unresolved dependency was the external FroDock distribution, including the adapter's scoring potential, rather than failure of the PINDER/RCSB endpoints.
- The available/partial split arose from classification and completeness judgements about substantially the same evidence. Both available-labelled draws still recorded the external baseline blocker.
- The lab's site moved its tool listings to a GitHub documentation repository that does not itself provide the required package; a web docking server does not replace a locally controlled comparator.
- No conclusion about GPU-box reachability follows from these local checks.

Differences and added evidence:

- Opus classified the prerequisite as `data_and_licence_evidence`. Here it is `data`: unknown licence evidence does not block current intake. An actual agreement/account condition remains owner action for any route that requires it; this is distinct from treating unknown licence as a gate failure.
- Opus could not parse the index, test ZIP CRCs or verify every case. Those checks now pass, including 3,590 unique structural/mapping/API files. The archive entry-count difference is confirmed to be exactly one directory entry.
- All six independently redownloaded index/metadata/test-ZIP/AAV SHA-256 values equal the hashes in Opus's report. This establishes continuity between these reader checks, not historical-study identity.
- Publisher manifests provided MD5 checksums that were not used in the earlier report; these now establish per-file size/integrity against the current official distribution.
- The original official acquisition page was recovered from Wayback, confirming the historical download agreement condition and its version label. No archive containing the actual required package was established.
- A third-party package is now reachable from a public GCS location, discovered through a separate project's Dockerfile. It remains unverified; the earlier absence of an available official distribution remains unresolved.

## Phase B: alternatives, provenance and verdicts

Search began only after the independent status. Sources were considered in the requested preference order. Searches and metadata checks were read-only. No login, paywall, terms acceptance, access control or rate limit was bypassed. In particular, no archived Download action was followed, and the publisher article's HTTP 403 was not retried through protected paths.

| Candidate | Provenance level considered | Evidence and verdict |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Lab's current tools page → author GitHub repository | `official_new_location` | [Current tools page](https://chaconlab.org/tools/) links FRODOCK to [chaconlab/FRODOCK](https://github.com/chaconlab/FRODOCK). API tree at `8bc416f66e6c0c0427576218d7c3336853906ed5` contains README, guide and image assets; release/tag lists are empty. No program archive, source tree or `soap.bin`. Documentation route confirmed; materials replacement rejected. |
| Author web server | `official_new_location` | [frodock.chaconlab.org](https://frodock.chaconlab.org/) is reachable. It offers remote jobs, not the locally controlled distributed program/scoring package. Substituting it would alter execution, hardware/control and measurements. No jobs were submitted. Rejected. |
| Original official download-page capture | `archive_snapshot` | [Wayback capture, 2026-07-10 16:37:51 UTC](https://web.archive.org/web/20260710163751/https://chaconlab.org/modeling/frodock/frodock-donwload) was retrieved as raw archived HTML. It identifies release 3.12 (2020) and conditions its Download action on agreement acceptance. This is a page capture, **not a package capture**. Its download action was not invoked. Insufficient for materials. |
| Wayback package search from original official domain | `archive_snapshot` | Exact download-action metadata, candidate static-tar URL metadata, component-prefix binary metadata, and a domain-wide FRODOCK URL query did not establish a program/scoring archive. The domain query first failed 504 and 503, then succeeded and returned 70 URL records. Download-like entries there are tiny HTML responses, not evidence of gzip archives. CDX `length` is archived-record size, not original tar size. No digest of an official package was established; none fabricated. |
| Software Heritage capture of author repository | `archive_snapshot` | The [original GitHub origin](https://archive.softwareheritage.org/api/1/origin/https://github.com/chaconlab/FRODOCK/get/) has a full visit on 2026-09-17. Snapshot `d2babf71b428e5286414d2fc89c8c4cd6ff4f708` points to the same documentation commit. Its root directory has README, assets and guide only. Origin search for `chaconlab.org` failed twice with 500 and then returned an empty list. No captured package/scoring input established. Rejected. |
| Author-linked DOI/deposit route | `author_deposit` | Official project documentation and the [paper's PubMed record](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/27153583/) identify the server; no qualifying program/scoring deposit linked from the paper/project was established. Zenodo's public API query `frodock` returned zero hits. Targeted Zenodo/figshare/author-name searches produced no qualifying author distribution. A PPDbench result concerns another benchmark and is not this authors' software deposit. The publisher article returned 403, limiting full-paper checking. This is a bounded negative finding, not proof that no deposit exists. |
| Public GCS package named by independent GCP project's Dockerfile | `unverified_mirror` | [Dockerfile](https://github.com/antonpp/frodock-on-gcp/blob/main/docker-build/Dockerfile) points to [frodock3_linux64.tgz](https://storage.googleapis.com/gke-dja-demo_static_assets/frodock3_linux64.tgz). Complete 26,637,000-byte gzip/tar downloaded without an account or agreement prompt at that public mirror. Expected program files and scoring input exist. No publisher/author-project link establishing this as an official relocation, no authoritative upstream checksum, and no matching level-1–3 package copy were established. **Rejected for usability under level-5 rule.** |
| Scipion distribution listing and package | `unverified_mirror` candidate | Search index lists [Scipion directory](https://scipion.cnb.csic.es/downloads/scipion/software/em/) and `frodock3_linux64.tgz`, with rounded size 25M and 2021 timestamp. Both listing and package failed four local attempts with curl exit 28, HTTP 0, zero bytes. Search metadata is not a successful current download, exact size, checksum or author-deposit proof. Not usable. |
| Public container built by independent GCP project | `unverified_mirror` candidate | [Docker Hub tag metadata](https://hub.docker.com/v2/repositories/antonpopovine/frodock-mpi/tags?page_size=100) reports one amd64 image, 347,851,638 bytes, digest `sha256:89650bbca09bb1d86302af6d222f72bbb65fd59c94220aff6cf6ed401e30e8e8`. Its Dockerfile retrieves the same unverified GCS tar and compiles it. The image was not pulled or run. A container digest identifies that image, not the author's original package or published benchmark environment; no qualification to levels 1–4. Rejected. |
| SBGrid consortium distribution | `unverified_mirror` candidate; access needed | [Catalog](https://sbgrid.org/software/titles/frodock) lists Linux versions 2.1, 2.0, 1.04, rather than the 3-series package layout expected by PINDER. [Access page](https://sbgrid.org/software/access-software/) describes consortium-member installation. No account or installer was used. Version differences can affect scoring and measurements, and no authoritative equality proof was found. Rejected; account access alone would not resolve equivalence. |

### Retrieved third-party package: exact evidence

The public GCS mirror transfer completed **2026-10-31 00:39:08.129329 UTC**, beginning 00:39:06 UTC. It was discovered from an independent project's build recipe, not from the original authors or PINDER's brief/intake links. It is never represented here as the original distribution.

| Object | Bytes | SHA-256 |
| --- | ---: | --- |
| GCS `frodock3_linux64.tgz` | 26637000 | `91b9aeff747346046c7f8c4c92fa5749c9f082d8122ee9d2d4769855d35368f4` |
| Its `bin/frodock_mpi_gcc` | 7169224 | `90550be435ba2239d030d84f7bb0dc5ed033e038cf3a22a678353975a303d557` |
| Its `bin/soap.bin` | 7536604 | `791f31ec8ef1ef1466092b425638e926755519c9626f5678ca675ef6c76234fc` |

The tar has 542 members and 65,507,120 uncompressed regular-file bytes; gzip CRC validation passed. The README self-identifies FroDock 3.12, dated February 8, 2020, and expected root name/compilation script are present. These facts are compatible with the old download page's rounded 25.40-MB label and 3.12 version, **but do not prove byte identity**. No exact historical FroDock version/hash was pinned by the intake records. The archive filename `.tgz` is gzip-compressed tar, like the expected `.tar.gz` format; filename/layout compatibility alone cannot authenticate it.

The archive also contains a different `src/frodockonstraint/Release_intel/soap.bin` (3,014,640 bytes, SHA-256 `6c791aad8ef72a600a9388adfbfda83b4d998cc87023d00c70cf0f2a9bb06948`). It was not substituted for the adapter's `bin/soap.bin`. Exact internal-file evidence is in [third_party_package.json](third_party_package.json). No executable or compilation script was run.

The mirror's own GCS MD5 header matches downloaded bytes (`1b13acacf8288471ee7cb8e26e29c3ac`). This establishes transport integrity **against that mirror's assertion only**. It is not an authoritative upstream checksum and does not promote the package to `verified_mirror`. Searches for that digest, exact filename plus checksum terms, and original-location captures did not establish independent upstream identity. Unknown differences in code or scoring bytes could change measured results, so they cannot be declared harmless.

## What remains unresolved

1. An authenticated, complete FroDock/scoring distribution at an original or level-1–4 location. A publisher hash/reference copy could resolve the public GCS candidate's identity; a matching filename, rounded size, README or third-party transport checksum cannot.
2. Acquisition via any route requiring agreement acceptance/account access must be handled by the owner. The historical page condition is now directly observed, but no licence/permission conclusion is made.
3. Separate implementation matters remain for later pipeline stages: PINDER installation/adapter validation, fixed candidate/seed schedule, AAV assembly/domain extraction, runtime/memory measurements and hardware-specific fetches. They were not replaced by fabricated inputs or protocol edits, and are not counted as newly missing source data.

The run5 brief addendum condition was not met. Both statuses have exactly the requested seven keys and enum/Boolean values; the independent one remains unchanged. Download cache paths in evidence are audit references only, not proposed run5 fetch locations.

The analysis-only pip environment used pandas/pyarrow/gemmi in the authorized cache; those inspection dependencies were not proposed benchmark inputs or a benchmark install.

Queries covered: FRODOCK 3/Linux64 download; exact `frodock3_linux64` and `.tgz`; official Chacón lab download; Wayback/Software Heritage; Zenodo/figshare plus FroDock/author names; SBGrid; and the downloaded mirror MD5 plus checksum terms. Search results supplied candidate URLs and stale original-page text; current reachability/completeness decisions use the logged local fetches.
